Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any biophysics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 249. The projective resolution has length at most 807 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 249 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the biophysics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 807. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
